### [1] [A geodesic distance interpretation of Lanczos coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16982)
*Le-Chen Qu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了大N随机矩阵理论中无限温度热场双态（TFD态）的展开复杂度（spread complexity），并揭示了其Lanczos系数的物理意义。通过库仑气体方程推导出Lanczos方程（非线性关系），证明Lanczos系数可作为平面地图的生成函数，并指出展开复杂度加速算子等于两倍的测地线生成算子。结果在四次矩阵模型和双标度SYK模型中得到了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 展开复杂度是量子混沌和全息原理中的重要概念，但对其在大N随机矩阵理论框架下的物理内涵，尤其是Lanczos系数的几何/组合解释尚不清楚。作者试图填补这一空白，建立随机矩阵、正交多项式、平面地图与展开复杂度之间的联系。

Method: 1) 利用态密度定义一族正交多项式，其递推系数与Krylov动力学的Lanczos系数一致；2) 从库仑气体方程出发推导出Lanczos系数的非线性递推关系（Lanczos方程）；3) 论证Lanczos系数作为平面地图的生成函数，特别是b_{n+1}^2-b_n^2生成两条腿的平面地图，其标记腿之间的测地距离恰为n；4) 在四次矩阵模型和双标度SYK模型中验证这些结果。

Result: Lanczos系数在大Krylov指数下趋近常数，导致展开复杂度在晚期呈线性增长。展开复杂度的加速算子等于两倍测地线生成算子。四次矩阵模型的耦合展开计数四价平面地图，双标度SYK模型的辅助希尔伯特空间可等同于物理弦希尔伯特空间。

Conclusion: 这项工作深刻揭示了展开复杂度、随机矩阵理论和平面地图之间的内在联系，为理解量子混沌系统中信息的展开（scrambling）提供了新的几何和组合视角。Lanczos方程的建立为研究展开动力学的普适性质提供了有力的数学工具。

Abstract: We study the spread complexity of the infinite-temperature thermofield double state in large-$N$ random matrix theory and investigate the physical meaning of its Lanczos coefficients. The leading density of states defines a family of orthogonal polynomials whose recursion coefficients coincide with the Lanczos coefficients of the associated Krylov dynamics. Starting from the Coulomb-gas equation, we derive a pair of nonlinear relations for these coefficients, which we call the Lanczos equations. Since the Lanczos coefficients approach constants at large Krylov index, the spread complexity grows linearly at late times. We further show that the Lanczos coefficients can be interpreted as generating functions for planar maps. For an even potential, the difference $b_{n+1}^2-b_n^2$ generates maps with two legs whose marked legs are separated by the exact geodesic distance $n$. Consequently, the spread complexity acceleration operator equals twice the geodesic generating operator. We illustrate these results in the quartic matrix model, where the expansion in the quartic coupling counts tetravalent planar maps, and in the double-scaled Sachdev--Ye--Kitaev model, where the auxiliary Hilbert space can be identified with the physical chord Hilbert space.

</details>


### [2] [Variable-mass sine-Gordon with point defects: integrability, soliton transmission, and quasi-conservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17211)
*A. R. Aguirre,H. Blas,H. F. Callisaya,M. C. de Oliveira*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有点缺陷的可积变质量正弦-戈登模型（vmSG），利用Lax和Bäcklund-规范构造了I型和II型缺陷矩阵，推导了缝合条件，并生成了无限层次的守恒荷。缺陷兼容性对变质量函数施加了匹配条件。通过参数α和β变形I型缝合条件，导出了相应的缺陷异常；当αβ=1时恢复一致的单孤子透射，αβ≠1时奇偶中心扭结-扭结和扭结-反扭结透射在最低阶显示消失的积分异常但在高阶不消失。数值模拟复现了可积透射/转换机制，并表明非可积缺陷产生弱辐射尾和最低阶准守恒荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具有点缺陷的可积变质量正弦-戈登模型，旨在理解缺陷如何影响孤子传输，并建立从精确可积性到电荷依赖准守恒的变形框架，为缺陷控制的孤子输运提供理论基础。

Method: 采用Lax和Bäcklund-规范表述构造I型和II型缺陷矩阵，推导缝合条件；通过缺陷兼容性条件确定变质量函数的匹配约束；引入参数α和β变形I型缝合条件以研究缺陷异常；数值模拟用于验证可积与非可积缺陷下的孤子透射和辐射行为。

Result: 构建了缺陷矩阵与无限层次守恒荷；最低阶在均匀极限下还原为标准正弦-戈登能量和动量；αβ=1时恢复一致单孤子透射；αβ≠1时最低阶积分异常消失但高阶不消失；数值模拟复现可积透射/转换机制，非可积缺陷产生弱辐射尾和最低阶准守恒荷。

Conclusion: 该研究阐明了精确缺陷可积性如何形变为电荷依赖的准守恒，建立了不均匀介质中缺陷控制孤子输运的框架，潜在应用包括非均匀约瑟夫森结、磁性和非线性光学系统以及有效分子和DNA模型。

Abstract: We study integrable variable-mass sine-Gordon model (vmSG) with point defects. Using the Lax and Bäcklund-gauge formulations, we construct type-I and type-II defect matrices, derive the corresponding sewing conditions, and generate the bulk and defect contributions to an infinite hierarchy of conserved charges. The lowest members reduce to the standard sine-Gordon energy and momentum in the homogeneous limit, while for inhomogeneous backgrounds they define integrability-generated energy- and momentum-type quantities. Defect compatibility imposes matching conditions on the variable-mass functions across the defect. An analytical transmission factor $z$ characterizes soliton transmission, topological conversion, and absorption/emission processes. We then deform the type-I sewing conditions by parameters $α$ and $β$ and derive the associated defect anomalies. Consistent one-soliton transmission is recovered for $αβ=1$, whereas for $αβ\neq 1$ parity-centered kink-kink and kink-antikink transmissions display vanishing lowest order integrated anomalies but no generic vanishing at higher order. Numerical simulations reproduce the integrable transmission/conversion regimes and show that non-integrable defects generate weak radiative tails and lowest order quasi-conserved charges, while quasi-conservation of the higher order charges remains unestablished. These results elucidate how exact defect integrability deforms into charge-dependent quasi-conservation and establish a framework for defect-controlled soliton transport in inhomogeneous media, with potential applications to nonuniform Josephson junctions, magnetic and nonlinear-optical systems, and effective molecular and DNA models.

</details>


### [3] [Composite worldline instantons and the nonperturbative particle decay in constant external electric and magnetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.12083)
*Alexander Gorsky,Ivan Poluboyarinov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文讨论了复合世界线瞬子（composite worldline instanton）方法在计算带电粒子在恒定的电场和磁场中非微扰衰变时的有效性。研究显示，在不同极限下，瞬子方法在指数主导近似下得到的结果与通过外部场中自能虚部和波函数重叠方法获得的结果一致。文章还探讨了将该方法用于估算质子在外部电磁场中衰变速率的可能性，并简要提及了末态粒子纠缠效应的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证复合世界线瞬子方法在计算外部电磁场中带电粒子非微扰衰变时的正确性，并为其在质子衰变率估算等实际问题中的应用提供理论基础。

Method: 通过将复合世界线瞬子方法的结果与两种独立方法——外部场中自能的虚部以及波函数重叠方法——进行对比，在不同极限下验证其一致性。文中采用指数主导近似（leading exponential approximation）进行分析，并讨论末态粒子纠缠效应。

Result: 复合世界线瞬子方法在指数主导近似下，于不同极限中与自能虚部方法和波函数重叠方法得到的结果一致，验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 复合世界线瞬子方法可有效地描述带电粒子在恒定电磁场中的非微扰衰变，并有望应用于估算质子在外部电磁场中的衰变率。末态粒子的纠缠效应可能对衰变过程产生进一步影响，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: In this paper, we discuss the validity of the composite worldline instanton approach for the nonperturbative decay of charged particles in constant electric and magnetic fields. It is shown that the instanton results in the leading exponential approximation in the different limits agree with the ones obtained by the imaginary part of the self-energy in the external field and by overlap of the wave functions. We comment on the possible application of our results for the estimation of the decay rate of the proton in the external electric and magnetic field in the leading exponential approximation. The effects of entanglement of the particles in the final state are briefly mentioned.

</details>


### [4] [Pure gravity OPE density for genus-two handlebody in $\text{AdS}_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16985)
*Saptaswa Ghosh,Sounak Pal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过极值共形场理论与亏格二环柄的对偶性，利用庞加莱构造直接反演配分函数，解析确定了第一阶和第二阶插值参数修正下的亏格二OPE密度。同时利用同一框架验证了亏格一结果与光锥自举一致，并证明极端CFT在黑洞阈值之上的窄带内，奇偶自旋态的OPE密度仍保持负值，这一病理在亏格二持续存在，并分析了密度从大正值到大负值的尖锐转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极端共形场论中，亏格一的OPE密度在黑洞阈值之上出现负值病理（即负性异常），这与物理上的正性要求相矛盾。作者希望探究这种病理在更高亏格（亏格二）下是否依然存在，以及高亏格下非平凡反演核的出现是否会改变这一行为，从而更深入地理解极端CFT与黑洞物理之间的关联。

Method: 利用亏格二环柄与极端CFT（arXiv:0710.2129）的对偶性，采用庞加莱构造（Poincaré construction），通过对配分函数的直接反演，解析计算了插值参数（pinching parameter）下第一阶和第二阶修正的亏格二OPE密度。作为自洽性检验，在同一庞加莱框架下计算了亏格一密度，并与光锥自举（arXiv:1906.04184）的结果进行对照。

Result: 亏格一结果与光锥自举分析完全一致，验证了方法的正确性。在亏格二下，尽管出现了非平凡的反演核，但OPE密度在黑洞阈值之上的极窄带内仍对奇偶自旋的态保持负值，表明负性病理在亏格二仍然存在。此外，沿某条密度为零的曲线附近，OPE密度经历了从大正值到大负值的尖锐转变。

Conclusion: 极端CFT的OPE密度负性病理并非亏格一所特有，而是在更高亏格下依然存在。这一结果暗示该病理是极端CFT内在的结构特征，与外尔引力/黑洞物理的深层联系紧密，并非由低亏格的简单性所致。

Abstract: In this work, we compute the operator product expansion density for the genus-two handlebody by exploiting its duality with extremal conformal field theories arXiv:0710.2129. Using a Poincaré construction, we analytically determine the first- and second-order corrections in the pinching parameter to the genus-two OPE density through a direct inversion of the partition function. As a consistency check, we also compute the genus-one density within the same Poincaré framework and find perfect agreement with the result obtained from the lightcone bootstrap analysis arXiv: 1906.04184. It is known that, at genus one, the OPE density of extremal CFTs exhibits a negativity pathology for states lying just above the black-hole threshold. We show that this pathology continues to persist at genus two, despite the emergence of a nontrivial inversion kernel. In particular, the OPE density associated with states of both odd and even spin $j_i$ remains negative within a very narrow band above the black-hole threshold. We further analyze the sharp transition from large positive to large negative values of the OPE density across a curve along which the density vanishes.

</details>


### [5] [Elliptic spin Ruijsenaars-Schneider integrable models from 5d $\mathcal{N}=1$ gauge theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17837)
*Gleb Arutyunov,Lukas Hardi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了5d N=1项链型quiver规范理论在R^3×T^2上的Coulomb分支与具有动力学非均匀性的椭圆自旋Ruijsenaars-Schneider模型的相空间相同，解决了该模型泊松结构和量子化的长期难题，并表明所得量子系统是4d N=1类S_k理论的超对称指标的支配可积系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆自旋Ruijsenaars-Schneider模型的泊松结构和量子化问题长期以来悬而未决。本文通过将其与5d规范理论的Coulomb分支建立联系，提供了一条自然解决该问题的途径。

Method: 作者将5d N=1项链型quiver规范理论紧凑化到R^3×T^2上，将其Coulomb分支几何与椭圆自旋Ruijsenaars-Schneider模型的相空间进行识别，从而赋予该可积模型的泊松结构，并通过规范理论的量子化自然得到量子化的可积系统。

Result: 成功建立了5d规范理论Coulomb分支与椭圆自旋RS模型相空间之间的等价关系，给出了该经典模型的一个自然的泊松结构和量子化方案，并展示了该量子系统正是4d N=1类S_k理论超对称指标的支配可积系统。

Conclusion: 该工作通过规范理论/可积模型对应，解决了椭圆自旋Ruijsenaars-Schneider模型的经典与量子结构的核心问题，并揭示了其与4d类S_k理论超对称指标之间的深刻联系，为相关可积系统与超对称指标的研究提供了统一框架。

Abstract: We show that the Coulomb branches of 5d $\mathcal{N}=1$ necklace quiver gauge theories on $\mathbb{R}^3 \times T^2$ are identified with the phase spaces of elliptic spin Ruijsenaars-Schneider models with dynamical inhomogeneities. This identification resolves the long-standing problem of determining a Poisson structure and a quantization of these models. We further show that the resulting quantum system is the integrable system governing supersymmetric indices of 4d $\mathcal{N}=1$ theories of class $\mathcal{S}_k$.

</details>


### [6] [Bulk Criticality and Boundary Spectra in AdS from Matrix Product States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16992)
*Faizan Bhat*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文使用基于矩阵乘积态的张量网络方法研究反德西特（AdS）空间中的相互作用标量量子场论。作者开发了从全局AdS能级提取低能边界谱、通过AdS半径有限尺寸标度探测体临界性以及识别共形边界条件的方法，并将其应用于AdS₂中的标量φ⁴理论。他们非微扰地计算了最低非平凡的Z₂奇偶边界标度维度，定位了体Z₂对称性破缺相变，提取的临界指数和中心荷与二维Ising普适类一致。在临界点，他们确定了低能边界谱并识别出Z₂保持的自由/普通Ising共形边界条件，从而刻画了体和边界的普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AdS空间中研究相互作用量子场论对全息对偶（AdS/CFT对应）具有重要意义，但强耦合区域难以用微扰方法处理。张量网络方法作为一种非微扰数值工具，有潜力在AdS空间中直接研究量子场论的临界行为和边界性质，从而为理解体-边界对应关系提供新的计算框架。

Method: 采用矩阵乘积态（MPS）张量网络方法对AdS₂中的标量φ⁴理论进行非微扰计算。开发了三项关键技术：从全局AdS能谱中提取低能边界谱的方法、利用AdS半径进行有限尺寸标度以探测体临界性的方法、以及识别共形边界条件的方法。通过这些方法在非微扰层面计算边界标度维度和临界指数。

Result: 计算了Z₂奇偶对称性下最低非平凡边界标度维度；定位了体Z₂对称性破缺相变并提取了与2D Ising普适类一致的临界指数和中心荷；在临界点确定了低能边界谱，识别出Z₂保持的自由/普通Ising共形边界条件，同时刻画了体和边界的普适类。

Conclusion: 张量网络方法能够有效用于AdS空间中相互作用场论的非微扰研究，不仅能够计算边界标度维度等关键量的值，还能定位体相变并刻画其普适类，同时识别共形边界条件，为AdS/CFT框架下研究体-边界普适类提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We study interacting scalar quantum field theories in anti-de Sitter (AdS) space using a tensor-network approach based on matrix product states. We develop methods for extracting low-lying boundary spectra from global-AdS energy levels, probing bulk criticality through finite-size scaling in the AdS radius, and identifying conformal boundary conditions. Applying these methods to scalar $φ^4$ theory in $\mathrm{AdS}_2$, we compute the lowest non-trivial $\mathbb{Z}_2$-odd and even boundary scaling dimensions non-perturbatively. We then locate the bulk $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry-breaking transition and extract critical exponents and central charge consistent with the 2D Ising universality class. At the critical point, we determine the low-lying boundary spectrum and use it to identify the $\mathbb{Z}_2$-preserving free/ordinary Ising conformal boundary condition, thereby characterising both the bulk and boundary universality classes.

</details>


### [7] [Who Writes the Gravitational Second Law of Thermodynamics?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16997)
*V. R. Shajiee,M. M. Sheikh-Jabbari,V. Taghiloo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文定义了引力熵为与局部横向洛伦兹boost相关的可积表面电荷，绕开了传统对时空微分同胚的依赖，并通过类比牛顿力学第一、二定律的关系，证明引力热力学第二定律由特定的自由落体观测者写出，在此类观测者视角下，只要物质满足积分强能量条件，熵变严格非减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统引力熵通常依赖微分同胚对称性（如黑洞力学中的Komar电荷等），但微分同胚在背景无关引力理论中的定义存在歧义且未必与热力学一致。作者试图提供一种不依赖微分同胚、基于局部洛伦兹boost的引力熵定义，并证明其满足热力学第二定律，从而回答引力熵是否满足第二定律这一根本问题。

Method: 首先，通过局部横向Lorentz boost构造显现可积的表面电荷定义引力熵，完全绕开微分同胚。其次，类比牛顿力学：第二定律由惯性观测者书写，惯性观测者由第一定律定义；类似地，引力热力学的第二定律应由自由落体（测地线）观测者书写，且其测地线非仿射参数化满足非仿射度等于速度矢量场的膨胀。然后在该类协变定义的有因果性的自由落体观测者视角下分析熵变，并利用积分强能量条件证明局部第二定律。

Result: 证明了在特定的自由落体观测者类（其测地线非仿射度等于速度场膨胀）下，引力熵的变化严格非减，前提是物质扇区沿观测者路径满足积分强能量条件。这建立了引力熵的局部第二定律。

Conclusion: 引力熵可以以不依赖微分同胚的方式（基于局部Lorentz boost的表面电荷）定义，并且其满足局部第二定律，但第二定律的表达依赖于一类特定的协变自由落体观测者，而非任意观测者，这与牛顿力学中第一定律定义观测者、第二定律由这些观测者书写的结构相类比。

Abstract: We define gravitational entropy as the manifestly integrable surface charge associated with local transverse Lorentz boosts, entirely bypassing the conventional reliance on spacetime diffeomorphisms. Any notion of entropy must satisfy the second law. To answer the question posed in the title, an analogy with Newtonian classical mechanics is instructive: Newton's second law of motion is written by inertial observers who are defined by the first law of mechanics. We show that the second law of gravitational thermodynamics is written by free-fall observers with path parameterization in which the non-affinity of the geodesics is equal to the expansion of their velocity vector field. We then provide a proof of the local second law by studying variations in entropy as viewed by this class of covariantly-defined causal free-fall observers. We show that the entropy variation is strictly non-decreasing, provided the matter sector satisfies the integrated strong energy condition along the observer path.

</details>


### [8] [Statistically Steady Holographic Quantum Turbulence: Hyperuniform Vortex Matter and Crossover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17012)

Abstract: We study semiclassical geometries induced by turning on identical sinusoidal scalar sources on two asymptotic anti-de Sitter (AdS) boundaries. Varying the source strength, we find that large and small wormhole solutions appear beyond a certain threshold. Above this threshold, the large wormhole is always the dominant saddle in the two-boundary gravitational path integral; there is no regime where wormholes are subdominant. Under the ensemble interpretation of the gravitational path integral, this implies that once wormhole solutions exist, the putative ensemble has exponentially large fluctuations relative to the mean. This is unlike previously studied examples of wormholes sourced by inhomogeneous matter which have a region in parameter space where wormholes are subdominant, and therefore the ensemble is sharply concentrated around the mean. Interpreting the bulk geometries as holographic renormalization group (RG) flows, we also find that the sinusoidal modulation of scalar boundary sources results in effective holographic $β$-functions which are multivalued, indicating exotic RG flows. In particular, disconnected geometries correspond to flows that return to the starting fixed point in the infrared, while wormholes describe flows to a gapped phase in the infrared.

The results are structural characterizations, not independent physical derivations of context independence.

TL;DR: 本文分析了矩阵乘积态（MPS）算法中的基更新与Galerkin（BUG）时间积分方法，将其表述为针对矩阵乘积算子（MPO）表示的哈密顿量的一系列正则MPS扫描。作者证明了两种自然基更新产生相同试验空间的条件以及连续基之间系数传输保持所表示状态的条件，并在此条件下将已有的无压缩树张量网络BUG一阶误差界推广到交替端点MPS调度。通过与独立六格点计算对比验证了无压缩实现，并在16格点横场Ising和Haldane-Shastry模型上将BUG与双位点TDVP进行了比较。结果表明在相同时间步长和截断设置下，BUG执行更少的局部指数作用且有更低运行时间，但精度不等；运行时-精度曲线在Ising模型中交叉、在Haldane-Shastry模型中接近，说明方法优劣依赖具体模型而非普适。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Matrix product state algorithms must enlarge their bond spaces as entanglement grows and compress them to control cost. We formulate basis-update and Galerkin (BUG) time integration as a sequence of canonical MPS sweeps for Hamiltonians represented as matrix product operators. We show when two natural basis updates produce the same trial space and when transporting coefficients between successive bases preserves the represented state. Under these conditions, the existing first-order error bound for uncompressed tree-tensor-network BUG also applies to the alternating-endpoint MPS schedule. We verify the uncompressed implementation against an independent six-site calculation. We then compare BUG with two-site TDVP for 16-site transverse-field Ising and Haldane-Shastry dynamics. At matched timestep and truncation settings, BUG performs fewer local exponential actions and has lower runtime. These settings do not produce equal accuracy. The runtime versus accuracy curves cross for the Ising model and are close for the Haldane-Shastry model. The comparison therefore identifies model-dependent trade-offs rather than a general advantage for either method.

TL;DR: 本文提出QSimAdv，一种将可移植性定位在核函数边界之上的量子电路仿真框架。它采用延迟绑定（late binding）而非共享kernel作为厂商无关的基础，在表征、算子降低和数据放置阶段均按需绑定。在NVIDIA GH200和AMD MI250X/EPYC系统上验证，QSimAdv在N=32时优于Aer Hopper配置，在setonix上强扩展达到3.4倍（GPU）和2.8倍（CPU）加速，弱扩展可达256 ranks。

Conclusion: 结果表明可移植性可以位于kernel边界之上，同时保持native执行并扩展到分布式内存。QSimAdv通过延迟绑定而非共享kernel实现了跨厂商的高性能量子电路仿真。

Abstract: Portability in high-performance quantum-circuit simulation need not begin at the kernel. We present QSimAdv, which makes late binding, rather than a common kernel, the basis of vendor independence. Representation, operator lowering, and data placement are bound only when their required inputs become available. Before full-state allocation, circuit, noise, and output inspection can route eligible generic sampled-count requests to a stabiliser tableau; explicitly requested representations remain fixed. For full-state execution, backend constraints shape fusion; an ordered fused operator binds to a native lowering only after its physical targets are known. A first-class logical-to-physical layout map records non-canonical order across local and rank-address bits, so the dispatcher moves nonlocal targets only on demand. GPU, CPU, and Message Passing Interface (MPI) backends share these semantics while retaining native execution paths. We realize this design on NVIDIA GH200 and AMD MI250X/EPYC systems across local and distributed execution. With matched complex 32-bit floating-point state storage, QSimAdv leads both Aer Hopper configurations at $N=32$ and Aer's HIP backend at four shared MI250X sizes from $N=24$ to 30. Strong scaling exposes platform dependence: on setonix, QSimAdv leads both GPU and CPU comparisons at every measured rank, achieving $3.4\times$ and $2.8\times$ speedups, respectively, from one to eight ranks; neither the GH200 path nor the CPU path speeds up at eight ranks. Weak scaling reaches 256 ranks with 2 TiB GPU and 1 TiB CPU states. Together, these results support that portability can reside above the kernel boundary while execution remains native and extends across distributed memory.

Abstract: The proposed formulation establishes a formal correspondence between the Heisenberg representation and the Bayesian formulation of inverse problems by identifying the observational noise operator with the initial observable. The initial observable is linked to the quadratic likelihood norm in Hilbert space, which depends on noisy observations and a direct model parameterized by unknown parameters. Using the Karhunen-Loeve expansion, the quantum state is expressed in terms of the eigenvalues and eigenfunctions of the Matern covariance. The likelihood probability is then expressed in terms of the observed state, and the model-dependent state, and is rewritten as a quadratic norm in terms of the quantum states, where the observational noise covariance is proposed to be identified with the initial observable. The main results are summarized by four theorems: the unitarity of the evolution operator, the self-adjointness of the Hamiltonian, the time invariance of the observable, and the equivalence between Heisenberg evolution and Bayesian posterior probability. Limiting cases of the Matern covariance are analyzed as the length parameter tends to zero and infinity. Finally, numerical results support the analytical findings.

Abstract: Superconducting circuits (SCs) are the cornerstone of modern quantum technology, enabling scalable computing through coherent control of macroscopic quantum states. Abstract: Variational quantum sensing offers a promising route to high-precision parameter estimation, but its performance depends strongly on the circuit architectures used for probe preparation and measurement. Existing approaches typically optimize continuous parameters within predefined ansätze, restricting the accessible design space and limiting adaptation to sensing tasks and hardware constraints. Here, we introduce \textsc{AutoQSense}, a reinforcement-learning framework that searches circuit architectures using Fisher-information-based objectives. Numerical results show that the learned architectures recover known benchmark strategies, adapt to dephasing noise, and outperform fixed hardware-efficient ansätze while using fewer entangling gates. These results establish \textsc{AutoQSense} as a resource-aware approach to adaptive and hardware-compatible quantum sensing.

Transport therefore provides a model-calibrated proxy for entanglement without joint-state reconstruction. When two clouds overlap, the bath mediates a finite-range attraction, yielding finite-size evidence for a compact two-magnon bound state and a transient post-scattering signature. Abstract: Classical simulability is ultimately determined by both the dynamics of a quantum system and the observables being evaluated. Lie-algebraic simulation exploits the latter to make exact polynomial-time classical simulations by propagating observables through low-dimensional invariant operator spaces. However, its conventional formulation in terms of polynomial-dimensional dynamical Lie algebras does not directly accommodate bosonic systems as their algebras are neither compact nor semisimple. In this contribution, we overcome this limitation, making bosonic systems accessible to the Lie-algebraic programme of exact polynomial-time classical simulation. This recovers Gaussian quantum optics and extends it to non-Gaussian input states, while identifying exact polynomial regimes of interacting non-Gaussian dynamics including bounded-photon Kerr and pair-hopping Hamiltonians and nilpotent polynomial phase dynamics. We show that unlike in the finite-dimensional spin and fermionic setting treated previously, a finite-dimensional bosonic generator algebra alone does not guarantee finite observable dynamics. We further derive a controlled perturbative hierarchy for squeezing beyond exact sector confinement and confirm the predicted error orders numerically. We also evaluate operator spreading on interacting chains of up to $400$ modes and connect a topological doublon band with flux-reversed edge motion. These results provide a unified formalism for classifying, discovering, and systematically approximating tractable bosonic quantum dynamics with classical polynomial-time simulation.

Here we identify a complementary geometric requirement for collective ensemble Floquet control: disorder realizations must share a common Floquet eigenframe. Finally, we demonstrate a harmonic-free continuous-drive AC magnetometry protocol whose collective single-tone response is enabled by the synchronized Floquet eigenframe. These results establish Floquet eigenframe synchronization as a measurable condition for disorder-resilient collective control and quantum sensing.

We validate the circuit by reproducing the expected search behavior for single and multiple marked vertices and by analyzing the effect of the self-loop weight on the success probability. We further evaluate the implementation under realistic noisy settings using superconducting hardware's noise models and apply noise-mitigation techniques to improve the measured search performance. Logical-resource analysis shows that, for grids ranging from $8\times8$ to $64\times64$, the algorithmic register increases from 9 to 15 qubits, while the transpiled gate count increases from $3.63\times10^{5}$ to $4.38\times10^{6}$ and the circuit depth from $2.13\times10^{5}$ to $2.56\times10^{6}$. Abstract: Neutral-atom arrays have rapidly advanced to support thousands of qubits and execute high-fidelity logical operations. Unlike MoE games, OTG games select players individually at random according to a specified probability distribution, thereby probing each player's correlation with the referee. We show that, despite monogamy constraints, players sharing certain entangled states can moderately outperform those relying only on classical uncertainty. This advantage arises even in simple OTG games involving only Pauli measurements on qubits, where optimal entanglement increases the winning probability by at least 4%. This contrasts with MoE games, where shared entanglement has been shown in several settings to provide only limited (if any) advantage over classical strategies. We further establish a majorization property: the value of an OTG game respects the majorization ordering of the player-selection probability distribution. We also analyze in detail a two-player OTG game in which the referee measures one of the three Pauli observables on a qubit, and show that it is optimally played using a specific parameterized family of three-qubit $W$-like states. These results suggest that OTG games provide a useful framework for investigating the usefulness of multipartite entanglement in multiparty quantum correlations.

Abstract: A measurement of a single-qubit quantum sensor reveals at most 1 bit of information about the signal that was sensed. Quantum computational sensing (QCS) is an alternative approach that breaks with the paradigm of first obtaining a classical estimate of the signal and then computing a function of the estimated signal in postprocessing. Our results illustrate how quantum computing can enhance quantum sensing even with a minimally sized quantum system subject to the practical limitations of decoherence and error-prone operations.

The total form vanishes precisely on those outcome features that can be interpreted, relative to the prior, as revealing pre-existing properties; in particular, it vanishes identically if and only if the POVM is sharp and commutes with the prior. Finally, under maps that preserve the prior and are covariant under its modular group, asymmetry cannot increase, and any loss of asymmetry is offset by at least as much unsharpness, so that total non-objectivity cannot decrease.

Abstract: High-harmonic generation (HHG) is conventionally understood to produce attosecond pulse trains only when the emitted harmonics are mutually phase-locked, in accordance with the classical theory of mode-locking. This requirement, however, can be circumvented entirely for harmonic combs exhibiting intermodal entanglement. Frame-indexed causal models associate potentially different causal models with different reference frames while requiring corresponding operational variables to agree runwise. First, we show that no empirically adequate frame-indexed causal model can jointly satisfy Independent Settings, No-Retrocausality, and Causal Lorentz Invariance. Second, it is established that this result continues to hold when Causal Lorentz Invariance is weakened to Lorentz Invariance of Causal Connections, which requires only the skeleton of the causal structure to remain invariant, thereby allowing the direction of causal influence to depend on the choice of reference frame. These results are discussed in the context of relativistic interpretations of quantum theory and nonclassical causal inference.

Abstract: As quantum processors scale to hundreds of qubits, execution speed is a critical performance dimension alongside scale and quality. While substantial progress has been made in benchmarking circuit fidelity, existing speed metrics often fail to reflect the sustained, end-to-end throughput experienced by users running utility-scale workloads. This shortfall is especially pronounced for layered, parameterized circuits executed repeatedly within classical-quantum workflows, such as variational algorithms and error-mitigated simulations.
  In this work, we formalize CLOPS_h (Circuit Layer Operations Per Second) as a holistic speed benchmark defined over layered, hardware-aware circuits. CLOPS_h measures the sustained rate at which the system executes physical layers, parallel slices of qubit-disjoint two-qubit gates separated by synchronization barriers. Because each such layer is one time slice of an N-qubit circuit, this rate maps directly to the execution rate of layered $N$-qubit circuits, connecting CLOPS_h to published device capability claims, and to the device-level throughput ceiling we formalize as Max Circuits Per Second (MCPS). CLOPS_h is obtained under layer-fidelity operating conditions, binding the speed measurement to an independently verified quality envelope, and it shares its layer decomposition with scalable layer-fidelity (LF) quality benchmarks, enabling coherent interpretation of speed and quality without conflating the two.

In particular, for $0 < α\leq 1/2$, the sample complexity for both entropies is $O(d^{1+1/α}/\varepsilon^{1/α})$.

Abstract: We analyze the possibility to construct propagators of flavor neutrinos, which are particles with indefinite masses. This kind of propagators was used previously while studying neutrino flavor oscillations in frames of the quantum field theory (QFT) based approach. Starting with the operator and the path integral formulations of the QFT, we obtain that the naive derivation of propagators for flavor neutrinos violates the basic principles of the QFT. Nevertheless, we can still construct the effective propagator of flavor neutrinos considering the mass mixing term as a perturbation. In this situation, the effective propagator arises from the solution of a Dyson-like equation. We use the derived effective propagator to rederive the quantum mechanical transition probability for neutrino oscillations in vacuum in frames of the QFT. The application of the obtained results to neutrino oscillations in background matter is also considered.

Abstract: The nature of the $X(3872)$ exotic hadron remains one of the most debated questions in hadron spectroscopy. A full molecular treatment would require coupled-channel dynamics and long-range pion-exchange potentials, which are beyond the scope of this work.

Method: 1) 计算热QCD中的精确树级散射率，并与常用近似对比，量化偏差；2) 将精确散射率用于计算部分子层面的射流输运系数q̂，分析其运动学依赖性的变化；3) 利用精确q̂与蒙特卡洛模拟中使用的最优近似q̂之间的差异构建理论系统不确定性；4) 将该系统不确定性转化为协方差矩阵，用于贝叶斯分析框架更新q̂的不确定性带。

Result: 高能热QCD树级散射与常用近似存在显著偏差，导致q̂的运动学依赖性不同。精确q̂与蒙特卡洛模拟中的近似q̂之间的差异被量化为理论系统不确定性，并构建了相应的协方差矩阵，可用于更新贝叶斯分析获得的q̂不确定性约束。

Conclusion: 现有基于近似散射率的蒙特卡洛模拟所约束的q̂存在偏差，本文通过精确计算提供了理论上改进的q̂，并给出了可纳入贝叶斯分析的系统不确定性框架，使q̂的约束更加可靠。

Abstract: We examine the behavior of tree-level scattering in thermal QCD at high energies and find significant deviations away from the commonly used approximations [Phys. Rev. D 44, 1298 (1991), Phys. Rev. D 44, R2625 (1991), Phys. Rev. D.77, 014015 (2008), Phys. Rev. D. 77.114017 (2008)]. These deviations in the scattering rate also affect jet-medium transport coefficients at the partonic level, leading to a different kinematic dependence of the transverse momentum broadening per unit length $\hat{q}$. As scattering rates and $\hat{q}$ are used by large-scale Monte Carlo simulations of jets in the quark-gluon plasma (QGP), such as [Phys. Rev. C 111,054913 (2025)], current constraints on $\hat q$ are biased owing to the approximations used therein. Besides theoretically improving $\hat q$, the differences between the $\hat q$ herein and the approximate $\hat q$ used in jet Monte Carlo simulations are used to construct a theoretical systematic uncertainty, which in turn can be employed to devise a covariance matrix for $\hat q$ and enables updating the uncertainty bands on $\hat q$ obtained by Bayesian analysis.

</details>

Abstract: Modern Bayesian theory-to-data comparisons for jet-medium interactions, such as [Phys. Rev. C 111,054913 (2025)], are lacking the careful accounting of theoretical systematic uncertainties included within their uncertainty budget. The behavior of tree-level scattering in thermal QCD is examined in detail, finding deviations away from the commonly used approximations. Deviations in the scattering rate affect jet-medium transport coefficients at the partonic level, leading to a more intricate kinematic dependence for $\hat q$ and $\hat e$ than, say, the well-known logarithmic behavior. These deviations away from well-known behavior are used to estimate theoretical systematic uncertainties in Bayesian analysis.

Motivation: 近年来NRQCD中导出的universal Lindblad方程（ULEs）为夸克偶素在夸克胶子等离子体（QGP）中的动力学提供了统一的量子描述，但其与半经典输运理论之间的联系尚未建立。本文旨在建立ULE框架与半经典Boltzmann输运之间的桥梁，并为夸克偶素在QGP中的半经典输运描述提供更一般和系统的理论基础。

Result: 本文首次直接从universal Lindblad框架导出了Boltzmann输运方程，该方程超越小偶极近似的限制，可描述重夸克对从紧致到宽分离构型的演化。与Yao等人的RWA结果比较发现：单态方程近乎完全一致，但八重态方程中额外包含一个描述八重态散射态连续谱内跃迁的碰撞项，该项在RWA推导中缺失。

Conclusion: 本文建立的universal Lindblad方程与半经典Boltzmann输运之间的联系，为夸克偶素在QGP中的半经典输运描述提供了更一般和更系统的理论框架，解决了RWA推导中八重态散射态跃迁项缺失的问题。

Abstract: Recently, a set of coupled singlet-octet universal Lindblad equations (ULEs) was derived within the framework of non-relativistic QCD (NRQCD) to describe quarkonium dynamics in the quark-gluon plasma (QGP). These equations provide a unified quantum description spanning the quantum Brownian and quantum optical regimes. In this work, we further develop this framework and establish its connection with semiclassical transport. We first derive the universal Lindblad equations within the potential non-relativistic QCD (pNRQCD) effective field theory and show that they coincide with the small-dipole limit of the NRQCD ULEs. We then derive the semiclassical limit of the NRQCD ULEs, obtaining a set of coupled singlet-octet Boltzmann equations. To our knowledge, this is the first derivation of Boltzmann transport equations directly from the universal Lindblad framework. The resulting equations are valid beyond the small-dipole approximation, allowing the evolution of heavy-quark pairs from compact to widely separated configurations. Taking their small-dipole limit allows a direct comparison with the Boltzmann equations of Yao et al. [Phys. Rev. D 99, 096028 (2019)], which were derived within pNRQCD from the Davies secular equation, relying on the rotating-wave approximation (RWA). While the singlet equations are found to be in near-complete agreement, the octet equation contains an additional collision term describing transitions within the continuum of octet scattering states, which is absent from the RWA-based derivation. Finally, we derive the leading quantum correction to the singlet Boltzmann equation. Our results establish a more general and systematic theoretical foundation for the semiclassical transport description of quarkonium in the QGP.

</details>

Here we describe a viable formation mechanism based on the enhancement of the local baryon density when supra-horizon Peccei-Quinn domain walls formed at an earlier epoch sweep and accumulate quarks and gluons before entering the horizon. Assuming an efficient baryon concentration by contracting Peccei-Quinn domain walls, we derive the resulting primordial quark pellet mass spectrum, minimum mass and cosmological abundance The results confirm that PQPs potentially constitute a conservative, Standard-Model-based, and observationally viable solution to the dark-matter puzzle.

Abstract: The leptonic decays of the $D_s^+$-meson have received considerable attention in recent years. In this work, we perform a precise calculation of the decay constant $f_{D_s^+}$ using the QCD sum rules method within the background field theory framework. In our calculation, we fully include the quark propagator contributions up to dimension-six condensates. By adopting two different constraint schemes, we obtain $f_{D_s^+}^{\text{(I)}} = 253.0_{-3.1}^{+3.3}\ \text{MeV}$ and $f_{D_s^+}^{\text{(II)}} = 251.8_{-1.3}^{+1.4}\ \text{MeV}$, respectively, both of which are in good agreement with existing theoretical and experimental results. The conventional scheme follows the standard Borel window criteria, while the derivative scheme reduces the dependence of the decay constant on the Borel parameter through an auxiliary function. Based on these decay constants and incorporating the NLO electroweak radiative corrections, we further calculate the branching fractions for the three leptonic decay channels for both schemes. Combined with the latest branching fraction $\mathcal{B}(D_s^+ \to μ^+ ν_μ)$ from the PDG, we extract the CKM matrix elements $|V_{cs}|^{\text{(I)}} = 0.967 \pm 0.012$ and $|V_{cs}|^{\text{(II)}} = 0.970 \pm 0.005$ from the two schemes, respectively.

</details>

Abstract: The origin of fermion masses and flavor mixing is often obscured by standard Euler-angle parameterizations, which mask the underlying connection between physical observables and Yukawa couplings. We prove that introducing a single, remarkably weak yet physically grounded commutation hypothesis, $[\mathbf{M}_R^2, \mathbf{M}_I^2] = 0$, on the Hermitian mass-squared matrix $\mathbf{M}^2 = M \cdot M^\dagger$ systematically reduces a general 18-parameter complex mass matrix $M$ down to a 5-parameter form per sector that admits \emph{exact} analytic diagonalization. The resulting mass eigenvalues are closed-form algebraic expressions that completely bypass the transcendental Cardano trigonometric reduction required for generic Hermitian matrices, while the diagonalizing unitary matrix $U_f$ depends solely on two parameters that form a dimensionless 2D geometric flavor-ratio vector $\mathbf{v}_f=(x,y)$. Combining two such sectors yields the physical CKM/PMNS mixing matrix directly as $V = U_1^\dagger(\mathbf{v}_1) \cdot U_2(\mathbf{v}_2)$, establishing a direct, first-principles analytical bridge from raw Yukawa couplings to fermion masses and mixing matrices without intermediate phenomenological inputs. Finally, we highlight that this exact leading-order baseline enforces an exact four-fold moduli degeneracy among mixing elements, pointing directly toward non-commuting extensions for full phenomenological precision.

Abstract: We investigate the axial Ward identity for massive fermions under a constant magnetic field at arbitrary strength, maintaining an arbitrary spacetime configuration of a perturbative electric field. We show that a spatially modulated electric field prevents the exact cancellation between the anomaly and pseudoscalar terms, generating a local axial-charge source even in the adiabatic regime where the frequency is subthreshold to massive-fermion production. Remarkably, unlike conventional magnetic responses, this charge generation is dominated not by the contribution of the lowest Landau level, but by those of the higher Landau levels. Our findings provide a microscopic foundation for anomaly-induced transport and anomalous optical responses in gapped systems. In particular, we find that the spatially modulated chiral magnetic effect in weakly gapped Weyl semimetals that exhibits a linear suppression of the magneto-resistance by the magnetic-field strength instead of the renowned quadratic suppression.

Abstract: Currently, the CP-violating phase $δ$ is the least precisely known among the neutrino mixing parameters. In the coming years, accelerator neutrino experiments such as DUNE and Hyper-K will measure $δ$, significantly increasing the precision. Such a measurement is usually performed by comparing the oscillation probabilities in the neutrino and antineutrino sectors, thereby directly observing the CP violation. This approach, however, has some downsides: in particular, it is more challenging to obtain good statistics in the antineutrino sector, leading to increased statistical errors. In principle, however, it is possible to determine $δ$ by looking only at the neutrino sector, studying the energy dependence of the oscillation probability. We investigate this possibility in detail; we find that, if this approach is used, the main issue would be the degeneracies with other mixing parameters, which affect the sensitivity to $δ$. Those parameters, however, have already been measured with great precision, which will increase even more in the next few years; if those constraints are taken into account by introducing Gaussian penalty terms in the $χ^2$, it is possible to achieve better results focusing only on the neutrino sector, rather than using the standard approach. Those degeneracies could also be broken by exploring a wider range of $L/E$: this can be achieved, for instance, by increasing the neutrino energy. In this way, the total beam intensity could also increase due to the relativistic boost (however, the conversion efficiency could decrease, affecting the total luminosity). We find that if the relativistic boost increases the number of events in the high-energy configuration, the best performance would be achieved in such a set-up; otherwise, the optimal approach would be to focus only on the neutrino mode without changing the beam energy.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Super-Kamiokande合作组最近以2.6σ的统计显著性报告了弥漫超新星中微子背景（DSNB）的可能探测信号。这一可能的发现为研究超出标准模型的新物理提供了一个新的观测窗口，特别是为探测轴子等弱相互作用粒子对原中子星冷却过程的影响提供了前所未有的机会。

Method: 基于长时程中微子-辐射流体动力学模拟，计算包含轴子冷却效应时的DSNB通量；将模型预测的DSNB通量与Super-Kamiokande观测到的通量进行比较，从而对轴子-质子耦合常数|g_ap|推导出1σ置信水平的上限约束。

Result: 获得了轴子-质子耦合常数的1σ上限|g_ap|<1.3×10^-9，这一结果与基于SN 1987A中微子暴得到的传统极限相当。与SN 1987A的约束不同，DSNB约束代表的是宇宙中全部核坍缩超新星群体的累积中微子发射，因此不依赖于单个超新星事件的特性。

Conclusion: DSNB约束为探测轴子和其他弱相互作用粒子提供了一条独立于SN 1987A的全新途径。由于DSNB反映的是宇宙超新星群体累积发射的统计性质，该方法的鲁棒性更强。更一般地，该方法可推广应用于任何能影响原中子星冷却过程的弱相互作用粒子。

Abstract: Recently, the Super-Kamiokande Collaboration reported an indication of the diffuse supernova neutrino background (DSNB) with a statistical significance of $2.6σ$. Motivated by this possible discovery, we investigate the impact of axion cooling on the DSNB flux on the basis of long-term neutrino-radiation hydrodynamic simulations. We compare the observed flux and our models and obtain a $1σ$ upper limit $|g_{ap}|<1.3\times10^{-9}$ on the axion-proton coupling constant, which is comparable to the conventional limit based on the SN 1987A neutrino burst. In contrast to the SN 1987A bound, the DSNB constraint does not rely on the properties of a single observed supernova, because the DSNB represents the cumulative neutrino emission from a cosmic population of core-collapse events. More generally, this approach can be applied to other feebly interacting particles that modify protoneutron-star cooling.

</details>


### [93] [Entanglement of back-to-back gluon pair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17685)
*Lei Yang,Wen-Hao Yao,Yu-Kun Song,Shu-Yi Wei*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了无极化质子-质子对撞中背靠背双胶子对的极化关联，计算了其密度矩阵。发现通过夸克-反夸克湮灭产生的胶子对在特定几何配置下呈现出显著乃至最大化的线极化关联和螺旋度关联，构成最大纠缠的贝尔态；而胶子-胶子散射通道产生的胶子对则处于可分离态，但仍有约11%的线极化关联。作者进一步证明可通过能量关联子的各向异性（cos 2ΔΦ调制）提取该线极化关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高能对撞中量子纠缠和极化关联的物理，特别是胶子对作为量子信息载体的潜力，以及通过量子关联特征来区分不同产生通道（q\bar{q}湮灭与gg散射）。

Method: 通过计算背靠背双胶子对的密度矩阵来量化其极化关联；分别分析q\bar{q}湮灭和gg\to gg两个产生通道中胶子对的螺旋度关联和线极化关联；利用能量关联子各向异性中的cos 2ΔΦ调制来提取线极化关联。

Result: q\bar{q}湮灭通道中，当胶子对在质心系中垂直于束流方向发射时，线极化关联达到1，且螺旋度关联在不同散射角下保持最大，系统处于最大纠缠贝尔态；gg通道中螺旋度和线极化关联均被显著抑制，系统为可分离态，但在中心散射处仍保留约11%的线极化关联。该关联可通过cos 2ΔΦ调制提取。

Conclusion: 胶子对极化关联可用于区分不同产生机制，并可作为研究高能对撞中量子纠缠的探针。能量关联子方法为实验提取胶子对极化关联提供了可行的观测途径。

Abstract: We investigate the polarization correlation of back-to-back gluon pairs in unpolarized $pp$ collisions and compute their density matrix. For gluon pairs produced through $q\bar{q}$ annihilation, the linear polarization correlation is sizable, even reaching unity when the final-state gluons are emitted perpendicular to the beam direction in the $q\bar{q}$ rest frame. Combining with the fact that their helicity remains maximally correlated across different scattering angle, the gluon pair system thus resides in a maximally entangled Bell state in this configuration. In contrast, for the $gg\to gg$ channel, both the helicity and linear polarization correlations are considerably suppressed, producing a separable state. Nonetheless, it still retains an appreciable linear polarization correlation of about $11\%$ at central scattering. Employing the anisotropy of energy correlators, we demonstrate that the linear polarization correlation of gluon pairs can be extracted from the $\cos 2ΔΦ$ modulation.

</details>


### [94] [VERaiPHY -- Validation & Evaluation for Robust AI in PHYsics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17724)
*Gaia Grosso,Ramon Winterhalder,Lydia Brenner,Louis Lyons,Tilman Plehn*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了VERaiPHY（人工智能物理学验证与评估）系列文章的开篇之作，该倡议隶属于PHYSTAT项目，旨在为物理学中机器学习技术建立统计标准。开篇文章奠定了概率、统计和机器学习的基础概念及贯穿全文的统一符号体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习在基础物理学中显著提升了精度、灵活性和计算效率，但在统计验证、不确定性量化和稳健性评估方面缺乏系统性的规范化处理，需要建立统一的统计标准。

Method: 通过VERaiPHY系列文章，从统计学视角针对各个方法学领域明确统计问题、检验方法及结果解释；开篇文章作为基础性文章，建立后续文章所依赖的概率论、统计学和机器学习基础，并统一全文使用的符号约定。

Result: 确立了VERaiPHY系列文章的框架和基础，为后续各方法学领域的具体文章提供统一的概念基础和符号体系。

Conclusion: VERaiPHY计划通过系列文章系统性地为物理学中的机器学习技术建立统计标准，涵盖开发、评估和部署全过程，以弥补现有ML方法在统计验证和稳健性评估方面的不足。

Abstract: Modern machine learning is leading to substantial gains in precision, flexibility, and computational efficiency in fundamental physics. Statistical validation, uncertainty quantification, and robustness assessment are less systematically addressed. The VERaiPHY initiative (Validation & Evaluation for Robust AI in PHYsics) is a series of articles developed within the PHYSTAT programme, aimed at establishing statistical standards for the development, evaluation, and deployment of ML techniques. Each article focuses on a specific methodological domain from a statistics perspective and clarifies statistical questions, tests, and the interpretation of results. This opening article establishes the probabilistic, statistical, and machine learning foundations that the later contributions assume, together with the notation used throughout.

</details>


### [95] [Bound-state spectra of $χ_{cJ}$ in finite nuclei and the universal pattern of mass levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17737)
*Tian-Le Gao,Ze-Hua Zhang,Xiang Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了χ_{cJ}（J=0,1,2）与原子核形成的束缚态，利用非淬火框架下通过D^{(*)}D̄^{(*)}虚循环产生的介质内质量位移构建χ_{cJ}-核势，并在局域密度近似下计算了6种原子核的束缚态谱。所有系统均得到束缚态，其中χ_{c0}和χ_{c1}谱近似简并，χ_{c2}由于更大的介质内质量位移而束缚更深。能级间距呈现近似A^{-2/3}的规律性下降，可用cosh型势统一描述，为未来JLab升级后的高统计近阈值光生实验提供了可检验的预言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索重夸克偶素χ_{cJ}与原子核之间是否存在束缚态，以期在核物质环境中研究中重子-核相互作用，并利用束缚态谱作为探针在实验中验证理论预言。

Method: 在非淬火框架下，通过D^{(*)}D̄^{(*)}虚介子环计算χ_{cJ}在核介质中的质量位移，并利用局域密度近似构建χ_{cJ}-核光学势。随后在6种不同质量数的原子核（从^{12}C到^{208}Pb）上数值求解束缚态薛定谔方程，获得束缚态谱，并通过cosh型势拟合分析能级间距的系统学规律。

Result: 所有6种核系统均存在χ_{cJ}束缚态；χ_{c0}(1P)和χ_{c1}(1P)谱近似简并，χ_{c2}(1P)因更大的介质内质量位移而束缚更深。绝对束缚能对截断参数敏感，但相对于1s态的能级差对其不敏感，且随核质量数近似按A^{-2/3}规律递减。cosh型势能紧凑描述这些谱。

Conclusion: 预言了χ_{cJ}-核束缚态的存在及其能级间距的普适系统学规律，为未来在升级后的JLab设施上通过高统计近阈值光生实验检验这些束缚态结构提供了理论依据。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate possible $χ_{cJ}$--nuclear bound states with $J=0,1,2$ using in-medium mass shifts generated by virtual $D^{(*)}\bar{D}^{(*)}$ loops within an unquenched framework. The resulting $χ_{cJ}$--nucleus potentials are constructed in the local density approximation, and the bound state spectra are calculated for $^{12}{\rm C}$, $^{16}{\rm O}$, $^{40}{\rm Ca}$, $^{90}{\rm Zr}$, $^{197}{\rm Au}$, and $^{208}{\rm Pb}$. Bound states are obtained for all systems considered. The $χ_{c0}(1P)$ and $χ_{c1}(1P)$ spectra are nearly degenerate, whereas the larger in-medium mass shift of $χ_{c2}(1P)$ leads to deeper binding. Although the absolute bound state energies depend appreciably on the cutoff parameter, the energy differences relative to the $1s$ level are considerably less sensitive to it and exhibit a regular pattern that decreases approximately as $A^{-2/3}$ with increasing nuclear mass number. A cosh-type potential with a common nuclear geometry provides a compact description of these spectra. The predicted bound-state structures and level-spacing systematics could be investigated in future high-statistics near-threshold photoproduction experiments at the upgraded JLab facility.

</details>


### [96] [Resolved Photon Processes: A Tribute to Rohini Godbole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17864)
*Manuel Drees*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文阐述了量子色动力学（QCD）框架下光子的强子性质：高能光子可涨落为夸克-反夸克对，从而获得类似强子的特性，并通过"解析光子过程"在电子-质子及电子-正电子对撞机中主导强子末态（如喷注）的产生，但其对未来高能轻子对撞机本底的潜在影响尚不明确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高能光子的强子性质及其引发的解析光子过程，对准确评估未来高能轻子对撞机中强子末态产生（尤其是喷注）的背景至关重要，而这方面的研究目前仍然不足。

Method: 通过量子场论分析，指出光子可在量子层面涨落为夸克-反夸克对；在足够高的能量下，这些虚夸克可通过与强子等粒子的相互作用转变为真实物理粒子，从而使能量超过数GeV的光子呈现强子特性。由此引入解析光子过程，作为探测这些强子性质的物理反应。

Result: 解析光子过程在电子-质子和电子-正电子对撞机上往往主导强子末态（包括喷注）的产生。然而，这些过程对未来高能轻子对撞机物理背景的影响仍缺乏深入理解。

Conclusion: 高能光子表现出双重身份——既可作为QED的规范玻色子，又可涨落为夸克-反夸克对而具备强子性质；由此产生的解析光子过程对未来高能轻子对撞机本底具有重要潜在影响，亟待进一步研究。

Abstract: Hadrons are particles composed of elementary quarks and gluons, which have strong interactions described by Quantum Chromodynamics (QCD); examples are protons and neutrons, which bind to form atomic nuclei. In contrast, photons are usually thought of as the elementary force carriers of quantum electrodynamics (QED). However, at the quantum level a photon can fluctuate into a quark antiquark pair. At sufficiently high energies these virtual quarks can become real, physical particles by interacting with other particles, in particular with other hadrons. In this way photons with energies exceeding a few GeV acquire properties of a hadron. Resolved photon processes are reactions that probe these hadronic properties. These processes often dominate the production of hadronic final states, including jets (sprays of collimated hadrons), at electron--proton and electron--positron colliders. Implications of this for backgrounds at future high--energy lepton colliders remain poorly understood.

</details>


### [97] [Can $γγ$ collisions rival $e^-e^+$ in probing doubly charged Higgs bosons?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18023)
*Abdesslam Arhrib,Rachid Benbrik,Mohammed Boukidi,Mohamed Chabab,Khalid Goure,Stefano Moretti*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在ILC和CLIC等未来轻子对撞机上，通过高能γγ碰撞探测双重荷希格斯玻色子（在2HDMcT模型中的可行性），发现γγ碰撞模式在4ℓ+E_T^miss信号上可媲美甚至超越传统e⁺e⁻模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索扩展希格斯扇区是超越标准模型的重要途径。双重荷希格斯玻色子（H^{±±}）是2HDMcT（带II型跷跷板的二重希格斯双重态模型）中的关键新粒子，但目前对其探测手段的研究主要集中在e⁺e⁻对撞模式，而γγ对撞作为ILC/CLIC的可运行模式，其探测潜力尚未被充分评估。

Method: 聚焦两个三体产生道：γγ→H^{±±}H₁^∓H₁^∓和γγ→H^{±±}H₁^∓W^∓，对参数空间进行了全面扫描，施加理论约束以及当前对撞机、味物理和电弱精确观测量（EWPOs）约束。在√s=830和1245 GeV质心能量下进行详细的信号-本底分析，评估5σ发现显著性。

Result: 发现γγ碰撞虽然有效光度因光子谱而降低，但其产生截面比e⁺e⁻碰撞高出一个数量级以上，足以弥补这一不足。在可行的基准点（BPs）上，可在√s=830和1245 GeV下实现5σ水平的发现显著性，其探测潜力可与传统e⁺e⁻模式相媲美。

Conclusion: γγ碰撞模式为探测双重荷希格斯玻色子提供了一条具有竞争力的新途径，可成为未来轻子对撞机ILC/CLIC探索扩展希格斯扇区的重要补充运行模式。

Abstract: High-energy $γγ$ collisions, realizable as an operational mode of future lepton linear colliders such as the ILC and CLIC, provide a promising environment to probe extended Higgs sectors. We investigate the sensitivity of such colliders to doubly charged Higgs bosons within the 2-Higgs Doublet Model with type-II seesaw (2HDMcT). Focusing on the three-body production channels $γγ\to H^{\pm\pm}H_1^{\mp}H_1^{\mp}$ and $γγ\to H^{\pm\pm}H_1^{\mp}W^{\mp}$, we perform a parameter space scan consistent with theoretical constraints as well as current collider, flavor, and electroweak precision observables (EWPOs). We show that $γγ$ collisions can rival the discovery potential of the conventional $e^+e^-$ mode for probing doubly charged Higgs bosons through a $4\ell+E_T^{miss}$ signature ($\ell=e,μ$). Despite the reduced effective luminosity resulting from the photon spectrum, the significantly enhanced production cross sections, exceeding those in electron-positron collisions by more than one order of magnitude, compensate for this limitation. By performing a detailed signal-to-background analysis at center-of-mass energies of $\sqrt{s}=830$ and $1245$ GeV, we demonstrate that a discovery significance at the $5σ$ level can be achieved for viable benchmark points (BPs).

</details>

### [98] [F-rationality of multiplicity-free subvarieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16999)
*Matt Larson*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在正特征域上，多重复合自由子簇（multiplicity-free subvariety）具有F-有理奇点，这一结果加强了Brion关于多重复合自由子簇正规且Cohen-Macaulay、且在特征0域上具有有理奇点的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重复合自由子簇是射影直线乘积空间中的一类特殊子簇，其Chow类在标准基下展开时系数仅为0或1。Brion已证明这类子簇在特征0下是正规的、Cohen-Macaulay的且具有有理奇点。但Brion的有理奇点结论依赖于特征0的假设，自然的问题是：在正特征下，这类子簇是否具有某种类似有理奇点的奇点性质？本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 本文采用正特征代数几何中的F-奇点理论方法。具体而言，作者证明多重复合自由子簇在正特征域上满足F-有理奇点的判别条件。F-有理奇点是正特征下有理奇点的自然类比，通常通过Frobenius分裂、tight closure或相关的同调条件来验证。该证明推广并强化了Brion在特征0下使用的工具，以适应正特征环境。

Result: 证明了在正特征域上，多重复合自由子簇具有F-有理奇点。这是Brion结果在正特征下的推广和强化，因为F-有理奇点蕴含Cohen-Macaulay性和正特征下的"有理奇点"对应物。

Conclusion: 本文成功地将Brion关于多重复合自由子簇奇点性质的结果从特征0推广到正特征，证明了F-有理奇点的成立，从而填补了正特征情形的空白。这一结论同时强化了原有的正规性和Cohen-Macaulay性质的结果。

Abstract: A multiplicity-free subvariety of a product of projective lines is a subvariety with the property that the expansion of its Chow class in the usual basis has all coefficients equal to 0 or 1. We show that, over a field of positive characteristic, a multiplicity-free subvariety has F-rational singularities. This strengthens Brion's result that multiplicity-free subvarieties are normal and Cohen--Macaulay, and that they have rational singularities over a field of characteristic 0.

</details>


### [99] [K-moduli of Fano threefolds of rank 2 and degree 28](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17113)
*Joseph Malbon*

Main category: math.AG

<details>

Motivation: 理解K-多稳态Fano三维流形的退化行为及其在模空间中的几何结构，是代数几何中K-稳定性与模空间理论的重要问题。具体刻画某个Fano三维流形族的所有K-多稳态退化，有助于完整描述相应的模空间分支。

Method: 通过系统地分析族2.21中光滑K-多稳态Fano三维流形可能发生的所有K-多稳态退化（degenerations），利用K-稳定性理论、模空间方法以及Fano三维流形的分类工具来穷举并刻画这些退化，进而确定模空间M^K-ps_{3,28}中对应分支的具体结构。

Result: 找到了族2.21中所有可能的K-多稳态退化，并完整描述了模空间M^K-ps_{3,28}中参数化该家族的分支的几何结构。

Conclusion: 该工作为Fano三维流形的K-多稳态模空间分支提供了完整的退化描述，推进了对K-稳定模空间整体结构及其边界的理解。

Abstract: We find all possible K-polystable degenerations of smooth K-polystable Fano threefolds in the family \textnumero2.21, and describe the component of the moduli space $M^\text{K-ps}_{3,28}$ which parametrises this family.

</details>

### [101] [Quantized Volume Comparison for Fano Manifolds, II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17397)
*Kaixuan Lyu,Kewei Zhang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了第二作者的量子体积比较猜想：对于光滑复K-半稳定Fano簇X（维度n），对所有整数m≥1有h^0(X,-mK_X) ≤ h^0(P^n,-mK_{P^n}) = C(n+m(n+1), n)，且等号成立当且仅当X为射影空间。更进一步，该结论实际上在切丛T_X关于-K_X斜率半稳定的条件下也成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决量子体积比较猜想，这是关于Fano簇上反典范线丛截影维数估计的重要猜想，具有深刻意义，涉及代数几何中K-半稳定性和射影空间的特征刻画问题。

Method: 作者采用喷注维数计数技巧（jet-dimension counting trick），将其应用于由H^0(X,-mK_X)诱导的子层滤过。然后利用斜举半稳定性条件，推导出所需的维数上界。关键创新在于证明对更一般的切丛斜举半稳定条件也可得到相同结论。

Result: 成功证明了h^0(X,-mK_X)在K-半稳定Fano簇上的上界估计，并刻画了等号成立时X必为射影空间。同时证明了该结论可推广至切丛关于-K_X斜举半稳定的情形，这是一个更广泛的成立条件。

Conclusion: 量子体积比较猜想得到解决，且结论被加强到更一般的斜举半稳定情形，这揭示了K-半稳定性与切丛斜举半稳定性之间的内在联系，为Fano簇的分类与Hilbert级数估计提供了新的工具。

Abstract: In this note, the second author's quantized volume comparison conjecture is solved: If $X$ is a smooth complex $K$-semistable Fano variety of dimension $n$, then for every integer $m\geq1$, \[
  h^0(X,-mK_X)\leq
  h^0(\mathbb P^n,-mK_{\mathbb P^n})
  =\binom{n+m(n+1)}{n}, \] and equality for one $m$ characterizes projective space. Somewhat surprisingly, the same statement actually holds whenever $T_X$ is slope semistable with respect to $-K_X$. The idea is to apply a jet-dimension counting trick to a filtration of subsheaves induced by $H^0(X,-mK_X)$. Then the slope semistability condition yields the desired dimension bound.

</details>


### [102] [Motivic nearby functors on perverse Nori motives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17412)
*Khoa Bang Pham*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了若干候选的motivic（单势）邻域函子在perverse Nori motives上是一致的，并给出了由六种运算表达的规范函子，从而推导出Kontsevich-Soibelman的motivic积分恒等式，同时在附录中证明了几何起源perverse sheaves的Beilinson等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作的动机在于统一不同的motivic近旁函子构造，并建立它们在perverse Nori motives范畴上的等价性，以便通过六种运算给出更直接的描述，进而推导出重要的Kontsevich-Soibelman motivic积分恒等式。这为理解monodromy序列和相关的几何结构提供了统一框架。

Method: 作者通过比较若干候选的motivic（单势）近旁函子，证明它们在perverse Nori motives上等价。特别地，利用universal abelian factorization定义的规范函子被重新表达为六种运算的组合。此外，在附录中证明了Beilinson等价性的一个版本（针对几何起源的perverse sheaves），该结果在全文反复使用。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 多个候选motivic近旁函子在perverse Nori motives上的一致性与等价性；(2) 规范函子可由六种运算明确表达；(3) 产生了perverse Nori motives上的monodromy序列；(4) 推导出Kontsevich-Soibelman的motivic积分恒等式；(5) 附录中证明了几何起源perverse sheaves的Beilinson等价性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了perverse Nori motives范畴上motivic近旁函子的统一框架，证明了其等价性并通过六种运算给出了显式构造，最终推导出Kontsevich-Soibelman积分恒等式。附录中的Beilinson等价性为该框架提供了坚实的理论基础。这项工作深化了对motivic近旁结构与monodromy现象的理解。

Abstract: In this article, we show that several candidate motivic (unipotent) nearby functors coincide on perverse Nori motives. In particular, the canonical functor defined via the universal abelian factorization admits an expression in terms of the six operations and yields a monodromy sequence on perverse Nori motives. We then deduce the motivic integral identity of Kontsevich-Soibelman for perverse Nori motives. In the appendix, we also prove a version of Beilinson's equivalence for perverse sheaves of geometric origin, which is used repeatedly throughout the article.

</details>


### [103] [A $k$-Dimensional Version of the Largest Intersection Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17771)
*Yuxin Lin*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文针对有限域上多个线性无关的超曲面的交集，提出了一个关于F_q有理点最大数目的精确猜想（涵盖射影和仿射两种情形，假设q≥d+1），并证明了r=2、k=m-2的特殊情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限域上多个线性无关的超曲面交集能够包含多少个F_q有理点，这是一个自然推广。此前已有相关的Beelen-Datta-Ghorpade猜想和零维猜想，本文旨在统一并推广这些结果，并论证其正确性。

Method: 作者首先提出了一个关于r个线性无关的d次超曲面（其交集至多k维）的F_q有理点最大数目的猜想。然后，通过将Serre证明的r=1, k=m-1情形的结果推广，作者证明了r=2, k=m-2的情形：若F_1, F_2为互素的d次齐次多项式，则|V(F_1,F_2)(F_q)| ≤ d^2 q^{m-2} + π_{m-3}(q)。

Result: 提出了一个涵盖射影与仿射两方面的精确猜想公式，其特例包括Beelen-Datta-Ghorpade猜想（k=m-1）和零维猜想（k=0）。并成功证明了r=2, k=m-2的情形，即两个互素齐次多项式交集的点数为d^2 q^{m-2} + π_{m-3}(q)上界。

Conclusion: 该工作为有限域上超曲面交集的F_q有理点计数问题提供了统一猜想框架，并验证了r=2这一关键情形，为完整猜想的证明提供了有力支持。

Abstract: Suppose we have $r$ linearly independent hypersurfaces of degree $d$ in $\mathbb{P}^m$ (or $\mathbb{A}^m$) defined over a finite field $\mathbb{F}_q$, whose intersection is at most $k$-dimensional. What is the largest possible number of $\mathbb{F}_q$-rational points in the intersection? We conjecture an exact formula for this problem in both the projective and affine settings, assuming $q\geq d+1$. The case $k=m-1$ recovers the Beelen-Datta-Ghorpade conjecture [2] and the case $k=0$ recovers the zero-dimensional conjecture in [15].
  Another interesting special case of the conjecture is $k=m-r$, which corresponds to the complete intersection of $r$ degree $d$ polynomials. The case $r=1$, $k=m-1$ was proven by Serre in [16] who showed that if $F$ is a degree $d$ homogeneous polynomial, then $|V(F)(\mathbb{F}_q)|\leq dq^{m-1}+π_{m-2}(q)$. We prove the case $r=2$ and $k=m-2$, that is, if $F_1, F_2$ are coprime, degree $d$ homogeneous polynomials, then $|V(F_1,F_2)(\mathbb{F}_q)|\leq d^2q^{m-2}+π_{m-3}(q)$.

</details>


### [104] [Witt rings, Pfister forms, and equivariant birational geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17821)
*Brendan Hassett,Yuri Tschinkel*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文研究有限群作用下二次曲面的等变双有理几何，并在等变框架下发展了Witt群和Pfister理论的类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的双有理几何中，二次曲面及其Witt群、Pfister形式理论已有深入研究，但当引入有限群作用（等变情形）时，这些理论需要新的推广。该工作的动机在于建立等变版本的工具，以理解在群作用下二次曲面的双有理分类问题。

Method: 在等变框架下发展Witt群和Pfister理论的类比，利用有限群作用下的二次曲面的双有理几何性质来构建相应的代数不变量。

Result: 建立了等变Witt群和等变Pfister理论的对应类比，为研究有限群作用下二次曲面的双有理几何提供了新工具。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典的Witt群与Pfister理论推广到等变设置，为带群作用的二次曲面双有理分类提供了理论基础和计算方法。

Abstract: We study equivariant birational geometry of quadrics with actions of finite groups and develop analogs of Witt groups and Pfister theory in the equivariant context.

</details>


### [105] [Symmetric Differentials on K3 Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17953)
*Frank Gounelas,Christian Liedtke*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明：在代数闭域上，K3曲面存在非零正次数全体对称微分当且仅当特征为p=2且为Artin不变量σ₀=1的超奇异K3曲面。在特征零情形，Kobayashi已证明消没。例外情形下，证明了每个正偶数次数恰有一个（至多相差常数倍）非平凡全体对称微分。此外还推广了Jang的定理：特征p>0的超奇异K3曲面同构于光滑四次曲面当且仅当p≥3，或p=2且σ₀≥3。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K3曲面上非零正次数整体对称微分（symmetric differential）的存在性是一个长期未解决的问题。特征零情形已由Kobayashi证明消没，但正特征情形完全未知。本文旨在完整刻画正特征下K3曲面上对称微分的存在性与唯一性，并同时厘清超奇异K3曲面与光滑四次曲面之间的同构条件，填补现有理论的空白。

Method: 本文采用代数几何方法，核心手段包括：1）推广Jang的定理，建立超奇异K3曲面与光滑四次曲面同构的充要条件；2）利用Artin不变量σ₀对超奇异K3曲面进行分类讨论；3）通过构造或消没对称微分来证明充要性，并在例外情形（p=2, σ₀=1）下显式构造每个正偶数次数中唯一的非平凡对称微分。

Result: 主要结果：1）K3曲面在代数闭域上存在非零正次数整体对称微分当且仅当特征p=2且σ₀=1；2）在该例外情形，每个正偶数次数恰有一个（至多相差常数倍）非平凡整体对称微分；3）推广Jang定理：特征p>0的超奇异K3曲面同构于光滑四次曲面当且仅当p≥3，或p=2且σ₀≥3。

Conclusion: 本文完整刻画了代数闭域上K3曲面正次数整体对称微分的存在性，将特征零的Kobayashi消没定理推广到正特征并指出唯一的例外（p=2, σ₀=1的超奇异情形）。同时通过推广Jang定理，给出了超奇异K3曲面为光滑四次曲面的完整判别准则，深化了对正特征K3曲面几何结构的理解。

Abstract: We prove that a K3 surface over an algebraically closed field admits a nonzero global symmetric differential of positive degree if and only if the characteristic is $p=2$, and it is supersingular of Artin invariant $σ_0=1$. Vanishing was previously only known in characteristic zero by a result of Kobayashi. For the exceptional case we show that there is a unique (up to scaling) nontrivial global symmetric differential in every positive even degree.
  Along the way, we extend a theorem of Jang and show that a supersingular K3 surface in characteristic $p>0$ is isomorphic to a smooth quartic surface if and only if $p\geq3$ or $p=2$ and it is of Artin invariant $σ_0\geq3$.

</details>


### [106] [Combinatorics of the Fourier transform: Stokes data, Gale duality and frieze patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17992)
*Jean Douçot,Andreas Hohl*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究傅里叶变换对复仿射线上一类具有对称不规则类的不规则连接的Stokes数据的作用，发现其由丰富的组合结构所支配，包括Gale变换、三性（triality）及Stokes-frieze对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解傅里叶变换如何作用于不规则连接的Stokes数据结构是该领域的核心问题之一。作者试图从Stokes过滤局部系统和Stokes局部系统两个视角出发，揭示傅里叶变换背后的组合框架，并建立与已有结果的深层联系。

Method: (1) 利用Stokes过滤可由其recessive或subdominant解空间完全确定的事实，结合Mochizuki的结果，证明傅里叶变换等价于通过射影空间中点配置的Gale变换交换recessive与subdominant解。(2) 揭示recessive解与Stokes局部系统之间的等价关系与Morier-Genoud-Ovsienko-Schwartz-Tabachnikov所建立的点配置、超周期线性差分方程与frieze模式之间的三性紧密相关，证明差分方程与frieze的系数（相差符号）与非平凡Stokes矩阵元一致。

Result: 建立了Stokes-frieze对应关系，由此证明傅里叶变换下的Stokes表示由其组合Gale变换给出，并得到了显式的闭式公式。

Conclusion: 傅里叶变换在对称不规则类的Stokes数据上表现为一种纯组合操作——Gale变换，并通过Stokes-frieze对应揭示了几何分析结构与组合/离散结构之间的深刻联系。

Abstract: We study the action of the Fourier transform on the Stokes data of irregular connections on the complex affine line with symmetric irregular classes at infinity, both from the point of view of Stokes filtered local systems and of Stokes local systems, and we show that it is governed by a rich combinatorial structure: (1) Observing that, in this setup, a Stokes filtration is fully determined by the data of either its recessive or subdominant solution spaces, and making the link with results of T. Mochizuki, we show that the Fourier transform amounts to exchanging recessive and subdominant solutions via the Gale transform of configurations of points in projective spaces. (2) We show that the equivalence between recessive solutions and Stokes local systems is deeply connected with the triality relating point configurations, superperiodic linear difference equations and frieze patterns obtained by Morier-Genoud-Ovsienko-Schwartz-Tabachnikov: Up to signs, the coefficients of the difference equations and friezes coincide with the nontrivial Stokes matrix entries. It follows from this Stokes-frieze correspondence that the Fourier transform of Stokes representations is given by their combinatorial Gale transform, leading to explicit closed formulas.

</details>


### [107] [A note on smooth quotients of Prym varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18000)
*Anatoli Shatsila*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了étale双覆盖的Prym簇上具有几何来源的伪反射（pseudoreflection）。证明了当基曲线的亏格g≥4时，所有此类伪反射的阶均为2。利用该结果，证明了对于g≥5，忠实作用在Prym簇P上且P/G光滑的几何来源非平凡有限自同构群G必同构于Z/2Z或(Z/2Z)^2，且后者仅可能在g≤7时发生。该结果改进了Auffarth、Lahoz和Naranjo的先前工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prym簇是代数几何中的经典研究对象，其自同构群的结构对于理解Prym簇的模空间和商空间具有重要意义。先前Auffarth、Lahoz和Naranjo已研究了Prym簇上几何来源的伪反射及其生成的自同构群，本文旨在进一步确定此类自同构群的精确分类，从而加深对Prym簇商空间光滑性的理解。

Method: 本文首先系统研究étale双覆盖的Prym簇上几何来源的伪反射性质，通过对基曲线亏格g进行分类讨论，证明g≥4时此类伪反射的阶必定为2。随后利用这一关键结果，结合自同构群作用在Prym簇上的结构分析，对g≥5的情形进行有限群分类，并通过亏格约束进一步限定(Z/2Z)^2情形出现的条件。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 当基曲线亏格g≥4时，Prym簇上的每个几何来源伪反射的阶均为2；(2) 当g≥5时，使P/G光滑的几何来源非平凡有限自同构群G只能同构于Z/2Z或(Z/2Z)^2；(3) G同构于(Z/2Z)^2的情形仅在g≤7时可能出现。这些结果严格强化了Auffarth、Lahoz和Naranjo的已有结论。

Conclusion: 本文完成了对Prym簇上几何来源伪反射阶数的完全刻画，并在此基础上给出了几何来源自同构群的精确分类，澄清了群作用下Prym簇商空间光滑的有限群结构限制，是对Prym簇理论中自同构群研究的重要推进。

Abstract: We study pseudoreflections of geometric origin on Prym varieties of étale double covers. We prove that if the genus of the base curve is $g \geq 4$ then every such pseudoreflection has order 2. We use this result to show that, for $g \geq 5$, a non-trivial finite group $G$ of automorphisms of geometric origin acting faithfully on the Prym $P$ with $P/G$ smooth must be isomorphic to either $\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$ or $(\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z})^2$. We also show that the latter case can occur only for $g \leq 7$. This sharpens results of Auffarth, Lahoz and Naranjo.

</details>


### [108] [Combinatorial Hodge Index Theorem for Polytopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18003)
*Jacob B. Wood*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明了Maxim-Schuermann关于射影环面簇相交上同调符号公式可以推广到任意凸多胞形（不要求顶点是有理点），并讨论了多胞形的Hodge指标定理版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 环面簇的许多不变量可以通过对应的有理多胞形的组合学来表达。BBFK引入了凸多胞形的组合相交上同调，Maxim-Schuermann计算了射影环面簇（对应有理顶点多胞形）的相交上同调符号。作者希望将该公式推广到任意凸多胞形，并探讨Hodge指标定理在多胞形情形的类比。

Method: 基于BBFK的组合相交上同调框架，证明Maxim-Schuermann的符号公式对任意凸多胞形成立（无需有理顶点假设），并进一步讨论多胞形的Hodge指标定理。

Result: Maxim-Schuermann公式被推广到任意凸多胞形，同时给出了多胞形的Hodge指标定理版本。

Conclusion: 通过组合方法，将环面簇中关于相交上同调符号的经典结果从有理多胞形推广到了更一般的凸多胞形范畴，并建立了相应的Hodge指标定理类比。

Abstract: Toric varieties can be constructed from rational polytopes, and several invariants of toric varieties can be expressed in terms of the combinatorics of the corresponding polytope. Barthel-Brasselet-Fieseler-Kaup (BBFK) introduced combinatorial intersection cohomology for convex polytopes, which agrees with the intersection cohomology of the associated toric variety when the polytope is rational. Maxim-Schuermann computed the intersection cohomology signature of a projective toric variety, corresponding to the case of a polytope with rational vertices. Using the combinatorial framework of BBFK, we show that the Maxim-Schuermann formula extends to arbitrary convex polytopes. Finally, we discuss a version of the Hodge index theorem for polytopes.

</details>


### [109] [Obstructions to intrinsic perturbation for $A$-hypergeometric series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.18006)
*Ryunosuke Nakano*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究了A-双几何系统中的有序负支撑族在伪指数处的内禀扰动问题。作者构造了一个阻挠模（两个余理想商的商模），证明了内禀扰动可以产生比环境扰动严格更小的系数空间，并给出了该模的有限表示及多种配置下的显式维数与余维数结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Okuyama--Saito提出了一个问题：在A-双几何系统中，对于伪指数处的有序负支撑族，关系格内的内禀扰动是否能够产生比环境扰动更小的系数空间？本文旨在回答此问题并量化两者之间的差距。

Method: 作者通过构造内禀扰动阻挠模（两个余理想商的商模）来度量内禀扰动与环景扰动的差距。该模的逐次对偶是环境系数空间对内禀系数空间的商。作者利用两条涉及Tor_1的右正合序列实现该模，并用两个反链给出其有限表示。对于齐次系统，通过有限维余核给出典则形式解空间与内禀级数张成空间的商，并计算其余维数；这些结果可转移到公共非奇异域上的全纯解。

Result: 证明了内禀扰动确实可以产生更小的系数空间。对于格秩1和2，且存在非零阻挠的构型中，包含一个有序列的构型，该例子解决了Okuyama--Saito留待解决的问题。对每个整数q≥1，存在格秩2的构型（具有连通的列拟阵和由约化Gröbner基向量生成的正常仿射半群），其阻挠模维数为q²，内禀全纯解的余维数至少为⌈3q²/4⌉，该余维数在固定格秩下无界。

Conclusion: 本文完整回答了Okuyama--Saito的问题，建立了阻挠模的代数结构理论，并通过具体的构型构造证明了在固定格秩下内禀阻挠和余维数可以无界增长，深化了对A-双几何系统系数空间结构的理解。

Abstract: Okuyama--Saito ask whether, for an ordered negative support family at a fake exponent of an $A$-hypergeometric system, intrinsic perturbation inside the relation lattice can produce a strictly smaller coefficient space than ambient perturbation. It can, and we measure the gap by an intrinsic-perturbation obstruction module, a quotient of two colon ideals, whose degreewise dual is the ambient coefficient space modulo the intrinsic one. We realize this module by two right-exact sequences involving $\Tor_1$ and present it finitely by two antichains. For a homogeneous system, we present as a finite-dimensional cokernel the quotient of the canonical formal solution space by the span of the intrinsic series, taken over all fake exponents occurring in the canonical formal solution space and all ordered negative support families, and we compute the codimension of that span; the presentation and the codimension transfer to the holomorphic solutions on a common nonsingular domain. Configurations of lattice rank 1 and 2 with nonzero obstruction include one whose distinguished collection is ordered; that example settles the case Okuyama--Saito leave open. For every integer $q\geq1$ there is a configuration of lattice rank 2, with a connected column matroid and with a normal affine semigroup generated by the reduced Gröbner basis vectors, whose obstruction module has dimension $q^2$ and whose intrinsic holomorphic solutions have codimension at least $\lceil3q^2/4\rceil$; this codimension is unbounded at fixed lattice rank.

</details>


<div id='math.SG'></div>

# math.SG [[Back]](#toc)

### [110] [Periodic orbits for perturbations of Zoll contact forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17578)
*Tom Stalljohann*

Main category: math.SG

TL;DR: 本文研究Zoll接触形式的微小C^0扰动，证明在给定域内必然存在穿过该域的周期Reeb轨道。作为应用，证明了在Zoll黎曼流形上，任意在给定域外消失的C^0小磁势对应的精确磁场，都存在穿过该域的周期磁测地线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题源于动力系统中研究接触形式扰动后周期轨道的存在性问题，特别是在Zoll流形上的磁测地线问题。这一研究将经典的结果推广到带磁场的更一般情形。

Method: 核心方法是通过Rabinowitz作用泛函的梯度流线和同伦拉伸（homotopy stretching）论证。作者将问题转化为研究哈密顿量在C^0意义下接近定义哈密顿量时，Rabinowitz作用泛函梯度流线的存在性，并对柱面分量在(0,0)点的位置施加约束。过程中需要推导精细估计以保证所出现的模空间的紧致性。

Result: 证明了任意充分C^0小的正函数扰动Zoll接触形式（在域外恒为1），都存在与给定域相交的周期Reeb轨道。作为推论，在Zoll黎曼流形上，对任意在给定域外消失的C^0小磁势，存在穿过该域的周期磁测地线。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了Zoll接触形式微小扰动下周期轨道的存在性定理，并通过磁测地线问题给出了重要应用。其证明依赖于Rabinowitz作用泛函的同伦拉伸论证和精细的紧致性分析，该中间结果具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: We consider the perturbation of a Zoll contact form on some prescribed domain of the underlying manifold by multiplying it with a positive function which is constant of value $1$ outside the domain. For every sufficiently $C^0$-small such perturbation we find a periodic Reeb orbit of the perturbed contact form intersecting the domain. As an application, starting from a Zoll Riemannian manifold, we demonstrate that for every exact magnetic field, with $C^0$-small magnetic potential vanishing outside some given domain, there exists a periodic magnetic geodesic intersecting this domain. The theorem is a rather direct consequence of a result which is of independent interest: For Hamiltonians sufficiently $C^0$-close to a defining Hamiltonian, we show existence of a gradient flow line of the corresponding Rabinowitz action functional with a constraint on the position of the cylinder component at $(0,0) \in \mathbb{R} \times \mathbb{S}^1$. This relies on a homotopy stretching argument for the Rabinowitz action functional, in the course of which we have to derive some delicate estimates to ensure compactness of the appearing moduli spaces.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [Cohomological rigidity of smooth toric Fano fourfolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17233)
*Suyoung Choi*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了：任意两个光滑环带Fano四维流形，如果它们的整系数上同调环作为分次环同构，则它们必然是微分同胚的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究环带Fano四维流形的分类问题，探究同调不变量在多大程度上能够决定微分同胚类型

Method: 通过整系数上同调环的同构性（作为分次环）来推断流形的微分同胚结构

Result: 建立了从整上同调环同构到微分同胚的充分条件，即同调不变量足以区分光滑环带Fano四维流形

Conclusion: 光滑环带Fano四维流形由它们的积分上同调环完全决定（在微分同胚意义下）

Abstract: We prove that any two smooth toric Fano fourfolds whose integral cohomology rings are isomorphic as graded rings are diffeomorphic.

</details>


### [112] [Shape Theory of $\infty$-Topoi: Inverse Limits, Products, and (Co)homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17525)
*Georg Lehner*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文系统性地建立了∞-拓扑斯（∞-topoi）形状理论，将其视为广义同伦类型，证明了形状的基本函子性质（保余极限、下降、同伦不变性），在紧致性和完全性假设下证明了形状保持余滤极限，建立了Künneth型公式，并给出了形状决定上同调与同调的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ∞-拓扑斯的形状（shape）是连接高阶范畴论与经典同伦论的重要桥梁，但目前缺乏对该理论的系统化处理。作者旨在给出形状理论的系统性框架，揭示其作为广义同伦类型的本质，并建立其在极限、乘积及（上）同调方面的关键性质。

Method: 作者以高阶范畴论和∞-范畴理论为工具，系统定义并研究了∞-拓扑斯形状的函子性质。具体方法包括：证明形状保余极限、满足下降性并具备同伦不变性；在紧致性和完全性（compactness and perfectness）条件下证明形状保余滤极限；推导乘积情形的Künneth型公式；并给出形状决定（上）同调的条件判据。

Result: 建立了∞-拓扑斯形状理论的系统框架，获得了形状保余滤极限（在紧致/完全性条件下）的结果，证明了Künneth型公式，并明确了形状决定（上）同调性的充分条件。

Conclusion: 该工作为∞-拓扑斯的形状理论提供了完整而系统的数学基础，确立了形状作为广义同伦类型的核心地位，并为后续研究∞-拓扑斯的同伦不变量（如（上）同调）与形状之间的关系奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We give a systematic account of the shape theory of $\infty$-topoi, viewing the shape of an $\infty$-topos as its generalized homotopy type. We establish the basic functorial properties of the shape, including preservation of colimits, descent, and homotopy invariance. We then prove that shape preserves cofiltered limits under compactness and perfectness hypotheses and establish Künneth-type formulas for products. Finally, we give conditions under which the shape of an $\infty$-topos determines its cohomology and homology.

</details>


### [113] [Local Inertness of Poincaré duality complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17563)
*Samik Basu,Sebastian Chenery,Ruizhi Huang,Lewis Stanton,Stephen Theriault*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了在某些同调条件下，庞加莱对偶复形顶胞腔的贴附映射在远离有限素数集合的局部化后是惰性的，改进了Félix和Tanré的结果。此外，还给出了满足特定假设的单连通6维庞加莱对偶复形的环路空间分解，并证明了在这些条件下(n-1)-骨架满足Moore猜想的双曲形式，从而构造了新的非惰性的p-局部球面映射示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究的动机在于推进对庞加莱对偶复形顶胞腔贴附映射性质的理解。Félix和Tanré此前已给出相关结果，但本文希望在更宽的条件或更明确的素数集合下改进这一结果，同时探索其下游应用——包括环路空间分解和Moore猜想。

Method: 本文采用同调代数与同伦论相结合的方法。核心工具包括：(1) 利用庞加莱对偶复形的特殊同调结构，在特定同调条件下分析顶胞腔贴附映射的惰性；(2) 在远离显式有限素数集合的局部化下进行分析；(3) 通过惰性映射的性质推导环路空间的分解，并验证Moore猜想双曲形式的成立条件。

Result: 本文取得三项主要结果：(1) 在特定同调条件下，顶胞腔贴附映射在远离有限素数集合的局部化下是惰性的，改进了Félix和Tanré的先前期工作；(2) 在满足特定假设下，得到单连通6维庞加莱对偶复形的环路空间分解；(3) 证明了(n-1)-骨架满足Moore猜想的双曲形式（在远离显式有限素数集合的局部化下），并由此构造了新的非惰性的p-局部球面映射例子。

Conclusion: 本文通过同调条件刻画了庞加莱对偶复形顶胞腔贴附映射的惰性，不仅改进了已有结果，还通过惰性理论建立了与Moore猜想之间的新联系，并为构造非惰性p-局部映射提供了新方法。这些结果深化了对庞加莱对偶复形同伦结构的理解。

Abstract: We prove that, under certain homological conditions, the attaching map of the top cell of a Poincaré duality complex is inert when localised away from a finite set of primes. This improves on a result of Félix and Tanré in these cases. As an additional application of the methods, we give a loop space decomposition of simply-connected $6$-dimensional Poincaré duality complexes satisfying certain hypotheses. We also show that, under the hypotheses of the inertness theorem, the $(n-1)$-skeleton of an $n$-dimensional Poincaré duality complex satisfies the hyperbolic form of Moore's Conjecture after localising away from an explicit finite set of primes, and use this to obtain new examples of \(p\)-local maps between spheres that are not inert.

</details>


### [114] [On the degrees of equivariant maps from spheres to complex Stiefel manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.17752)
*Haibao Duan,Ruizhi Huang*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究从球面到复Stiefel流形的Z/m等变映射的度数集合，在适当的算术条件下，利用James、Atiyah-Todd和Adams-Walker的结果确定该集合，采用同伦论方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Astey-Gitler-Micha-Pastor工作的启发，研究Z/m等变映射从球面到复Stiefel流形的度数集合问题。等变映射的度数在拓扑学和几何中具有重要意义，特别是在等变同伦论与流形分类问题的交叉领域。

Method: 采用同伦论的方法。在合适的算术条件下，借助James关于Stiefel流形的结果、Atiyah-Todd关于球映射的结果以及Adams-Walker的结果来确定等变映射的度数集合。

Result: 在特定的算术条件下，完整确定了从球面到复Stiefel流形的Z/m等变映射的度数集合。

Conclusion: 通过同伦论方法，在适当的算术假设下，给出了这类等变映射度数集合的完整刻画，推广了Astey-Gitler-Micha-Pastor的相关工作。

Abstract: We study the set of degrees of $\mathbb{Z}/m$-equivariant maps from spheres to complex Stiefel manifolds, motivated by the work of Astey--Gitler--Micha--Pastor. Under a suitable arithmetic condition, this set is determined using results of James, Atiyah--Todd, and Adams--Walker. Our approach is homotopy-theoretic.

</details>
